Cinnamoroll
-----------

像素素材來源 / Pixel credits
============================

sk_ = https://sakuradreams.neocities.org/Pages/Cinnamoroll （Sakura Dreams 的大耳狗像素收藏）
cp_ = https://caramelpuddinz.neocities.org/cinnamoroll （caramelpuddinz 的大耳狗像素收藏）

兩個站都是「從網路上收集來的」像素圖，原作者多半不可考；Sakura Dreams 有標明的原作者列在 [by ...] 裡。
Cinnamoroll © Sanrio Co., Ltd. 僅供非營利粉絲站使用。

Kuromi
------

酷洛米神殿 像素素材來源 / Pixel credits
=====================================

sk_ = Sakura Dreams（https://sakuradreams.neocities.org/Pages/Kuromi，另外從美樂蒂頁與萬聖節頁挑了幾張）
cg_ = cute.gif（https://cutegif.neocities.org/sanrio/kuromelo/kuromelo.html）
da_ = DeviantArt 上標示 f2u（free to use）的郵票／閃閃貼，作者見 [by ...]
bc_ = 用 blinkies.cafe 產生的文字閃閃貼（CC BY 4.0，https://blinkies.cafe/）
kr_ = 本站自製

Sakura Dreams 與 cute.gif 都是「從網路上收集來的」2000 年代粉絲站像素圖，原作者多半不可考。
Kuromi © Sanrio Co., Ltd. 僅供非營利粉絲站使用。

Keroppi
-------

Keroppi pixel collection — source inventory
Collected: 2026-09-12
Keroppi © Sanrio. The underlying character and individual images are not claimed as original work.
90s / early-web style describes the look; individual creation dates are not verified.
Archival copies are personal research references, not proof of permission or a commercial-use license.
Original bytes are preserved. Still previews are first-frame derivatives for the Pause animation control.

Hello Kitty
-----------

Hello Kitty pixel collection - source inventory
Collected: 2026-09-12
Hello Kitty and related characters are Sanrio characters. Original images are not claimed as our work.
90s / early-web describes the look, not verified creation dates.
Archival copies are personal research references, not proof of permission or a commercial-use license.
Original bytes are preserved. The source gallery has separate first-frame PNGs for pausing animation.
